Source: Google Scholar alert "Auditor Bias"

Filter: algorithmic auditing of AI/LLMs — methods, frameworks, benchmarks for auditing bias/fairness/behavior in ML models. Excludes accounting-auditor cognitive bias, generic AI-ethics commentary, and unrelated medical chart audits.

- Total unique papers: **980**
- Date range: **September 2025 – May 2026**
